```
1	/**
2	 * TEE lease-renewer round-trip suite (TEE-01, TEE-02, TEE-06 tombstone) — Phase 67.
3	 *
4	 * Two behavior cases exercised against the live API + migrated schedule-collapse schema:
5	 *   Test A (equal-seq / equal-CID / later-EOL): Enroll a record with TEE keys; make its
6	 *     schedule row due via a direct DB write; enqueue ONE 'republish-batch' job on the
7	 *     real BullMQ 'republish' queue (redis:6380); poll ipns_records.signed_record until it
8	 *     differs; assert renewed.sequence === original.sequence (no increment), renewed.value ===
9	 *     original.value (same CID), and the renewed bytes differ (later EOL).
10	 *   Test B (tombstone never re-signed): Enroll a second record with TEE keys; tombstone the
11	 *     name via POST /ipns/tombstone; assert ipns_republish_schedule row is absent (unenrolled
12	 *     by tombstone handler); enqueue ONE job; assert the signed_record is unchanged.
13	 *
14	 * These two cases need the live stack because they exercise the real relay→TEE→DB round-trip.
15	 * The determinism is fully test-owned: no timer/scheduler wait; exactly ONE BullMQ job per test.
16	 *
17	 * Prerequisites (live local stack):
18	 *   docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml up -d   (redis 6380, kubo, postgres, tee-worker)
19	 *   pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api dev                    (API on :3000, TEE_WORKER_URL=http://localhost:3002)
20	 *   pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api migration:run          (ScheduleCollapse1751000000000 applied)
21	 */
22	
23	import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
24	import { Queue } from 'bullmq';
25	import pg from 'pg';
26	import { parseIpnsRecord, deriveEd25519PublicKey, deriveIpnsName } from '@cipherbox/crypto';
27	import { type MultiAccountFixture, createMultiAccountFixture } from '../fixtures/multi-account';
28	import { API_URL, testFetch } from '../fixtures/test-harness';
29	import { createSubfolder } from '@cipherbox/sdk-core';
30	
31	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
32	// DB + queue connection settings (mirrors API data-source.ts defaults)
33	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
34	
35	const DB_CONFIG: pg.PoolConfig = {
36	  host: process.env.DB_HOST ?? 'localhost',
37	  port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT ?? '5432', 10),
38	  user: process.env.DB_USERNAME ?? 'postgres',
39	  password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD ?? 'postgres',
40	  database: process.env.DB_DATABASE ?? 'cipherbox',
41	};
42	
43	const REDIS_CONFIG = {
44	  host: process.env.REDIS_HOST ?? 'localhost',
45	  port: parseInt(process.env.REDIS_PORT ?? '6380', 10),
46	};
47	
48	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
49	// Shared fixture + DB pool
50	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
51	
52	let fixture: MultiAccountFixture;
53	let pool: pg.Pool;
54	
55	// This suite drives the real relay→TEE→DB→BullMQ lease-renew round-trip, which needs the
56	// LOCAL live stack: the TEE worker on :3002, the `cipherbox` DB, and redis. CI's sdk-e2e
57	// job provisions none of those (it uses `cipherbox_test` and runs no TEE worker), so gate
58	// the suite — and its module-level beforeAll DB query — to local runs. This is the
59	// documented local publish gate (see project-tee-republish-e2e-stack-recipe).
60	const SKIP_TEE_LIVE = !!process.env.CI;
61	
62	beforeAll(async () => {
63	  if (SKIP_TEE_LIVE) return;
64	  fixture = await createMultiAccountFixture(['alice', 'bob']);
65	  pool = new pg.Pool(DB_CONFIG);
66	
67	  // Guard (T-67-08-T): assert the schedule-collapse migration is applied — the four
68	  // signing-input columns MUST be absent. The round-trip below would still pass against
69	  // an un-migrated DB (the dropped columns would merely sit unused), so without this
70	  // check the suite could green on a stale schema. Fail loudly instead.
71	  const { rows } = await pool.query<{ column_name: string }>(
72	    `SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns
73	     WHERE table_name = 'ipns_republish_schedule'
74	       AND column_name IN ('encrypted_ipns_key', 'key_epoch', 'latest_cid', 'sequence_number')`
75	  );
76	  if (rows.length > 0) {
77	    throw new Error(
78	      `ScheduleCollapse migration not applied — ipns_republish_schedule still has signing ` +
79	        `columns: ${rows.map((r) => r.column_name).join(', ')}. ` +
80	        `Run: DB_DATABASE=cipherbox pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api migration:run`
81	    );
82	  }
83	});
84	
85	afterAll(async () => {
86	  if (fixture) await fixture.cleanupAll();
87	  if (pool) await pool.end();
88	});
89	
90	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
91	// Helpers
92	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
93	
94	/**
95	 * Read the TEE public key + current epoch from tee_key_state.
96	 * Returns an object compatible with the TeeKeys type expected by createSubfolder.
97	 */
98	async function readTeeKeys(): Promise<{ currentPublicKey: string; currentEpoch: number }> {
99	  const result = await pool.query<{ current_public_key: Buffer; current_epoch: number }>(
100	    'SELECT current_public_key, current_epoch FROM tee_key_state LIMIT 1'
101	  );
102	  if (result.rows.length === 0) {
103	    throw new Error(
104	      'tee_key_state is empty — ensure the TEE worker is running and has been initialised'
105	    );
106	  }
107	  const row = result.rows[0];
108	  return {
109	    // current_public_key is a bytea (65-byte uncompressed secp256k1); createSubfolder
110	    // passes teeKeys.currentPublicKey through hexToBytes, so hand it back as hex.
111	    currentPublicKey: row.current_public_key.toString('hex'),
112	    currentEpoch: row.current_epoch,
113	  };
114	}
115	
116	/**
117	 * Fetch the current signed_record bytes from ipns_records for the given ipnsName.
118	 * Returns null if no row exists.
119	 */
120	async function fetchSignedRecord(ipnsName: string): Promise<Buffer | null> {
121	  const result = await pool.query<{ signed_record: Buffer | null }>(
122	    'SELECT signed_record FROM ipns_records WHERE ipns_name = $1',
123	    [ipnsName]
124	  );
125	  return result.rows[0]?.signed_record ?? null;
126	}
127	
128	/**
129	 * Make the schedule row for the given ipnsName due immediately.
130	 * Noop if no schedule row exists (tombstoned names have already been unenrolled).
131	 */
132	async function makeScheduleDue(ipnsName: string): Promise<void> {
133	  await pool.query(
134	    `UPDATE ipns_republish_schedule SET next_republish_at = NOW() - interval '1 second' WHERE ipns_name = $1`,
135	    [ipnsName]
136	  );
137	}
138	
139	/**
140	 * Enqueue exactly ONE 'republish-batch' job on the real BullMQ 'republish' queue.
141	 * Creates a fresh Queue connection, adds the job, then closes the connection.
142	 * The processor (RepublishProcessor) picks it up regardless of whether it came
143	 * from the scheduler or from this one-shot add.
144	 */
145	async function enqueueRepublishBatch(): Promise<void> {
146	  const queue = new Queue('republish', { connection: REDIS_CONFIG });
147	  try {
148	    await queue.add('republish-batch', {});
149	  } finally {
150	    await queue.close();
151	  }
152	}
153	
154	/**
155	 * Poll predicate until it returns true or the timeout is reached.
156	 * Throws if the timeout expires.
157	 */
158	async function waitFor(
159	  predicate: () => Promise<boolean>,
160	  options: { timeout: number; interval?: number; label?: string } = { timeout: 15_000 }
161	): Promise<void> {
162	  const interval = options.interval ?? 500;
163	  const deadline = Date.now() + options.timeout;
164	  while (Date.now() < deadline) {
165	    if (await predicate()) return;
166	    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, interval));
167	  }
168	  throw new Error(
169	    `waitFor timed out after ${options.timeout}ms${options.label ? ` (${options.label})` : ''}`
170	  );
171	}
172	
173	/**
174	 * Check whether an ipns_republish_schedule row exists for the given ipnsName.
175	 */
176	async function scheduleRowExists(ipnsName: string): Promise<boolean> {
177	  const result = await pool.query<{ count: string }>(
178	    'SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM ipns_republish_schedule WHERE ipns_name = $1',
179	    [ipnsName]
180	  );
181	  return parseInt(result.rows[0]?.count ?? '0', 10) > 0;
182	}
183	
184	/**
185	 * Tombstone a name via the API (POST /ipns/tombstone).
186	 * Phase-66 tombstone endpoint — unenrolls the name from TEE republishing.
187	 */
188	async function tombstoneName(accessToken: string, ipnsName: string): Promise<void> {
189	  const resp = await testFetch(`${API_URL}/ipns/tombstone`, {
190	    method: 'POST',
191	    headers: {
192	      Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}`,
193	      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
194	    },
195	    body: JSON.stringify({ ipnsName }),
196	  });
197	  if (!resp.ok) {
198	    const body = await resp.text();
199	    throw new Error(`tombstone failed (${resp.status}): ${body}`);
200	  }
201	}
202	
203	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
204	// Describe
205	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
206	
207	describe.skipIf(SKIP_TEE_LIVE)(
208	  'TEE lease-renewer round-trip suite (TEE-01, TEE-02, tombstone gate)',
209	  () => {
210	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
211	    // Test A — equal-seq / equal-CID / later-EOL
212	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
213	    it('Test A: republish re-signs same CID + same seq with later EOL (TEE-01, TEE-02)', async () => {
214	      const alice = fixture.accounts.get('alice')!;
215	      const aliceCtx = alice.client.getContext();
216	
217	      // 1. Read TEE keys from the live DB
218	      const teeKeys = await readTeeKeys();
219	
220	      // 2. Create a subfolder enrolled for TEE republishing
221	      const { ipnsPrivateKey: subfolderKey } = await createSubfolder({
222	        name: 'tee-republish-test-a',
223	        ctx: aliceCtx,
224	        teeKeys,
225	      });
226	      const ipnsName = await deriveIpnsName(deriveEd25519PublicKey(subfolderKey));
227	
228	      // 3. Capture the original signed_record bytes + sequence
229	      const originalBytes = await fetchSignedRecord(ipnsName);
230	      expect(originalBytes).not.toBeNull();
231	      const originalRecord = await parseIpnsRecord(new Uint8Array(originalBytes!));
232	
233	      // 4. Make the schedule row due immediately
234	      await makeScheduleDue(ipnsName);
235	
236	      // 5. Enqueue ONE republish-batch job (deterministic trigger, no scheduler wait)
237	      await enqueueRepublishBatch();
238	
239	      // 6. Poll until ipns_records.signed_record changes
240	      await waitFor(
241	        async () => {
242	          const current = await fetchSignedRecord(ipnsName);
243	          if (!current) return false;
244	          return !current.equals(originalBytes!);
245	        },
246	        { timeout: 15_000, label: 'wait for signed_record to be renewed' }
247	      );
248	
249	      // 7. Fetch renewed record and parse it
250	      const renewedBytes = await fetchSignedRecord(ipnsName);
251	      expect(renewedBytes).not.toBeNull();
252	      const renewedRecord = await parseIpnsRecord(new Uint8Array(renewedBytes!));
253	
254	      // 8. Assert TEE-01 + TEE-02 invariants:
255	      //    - same sequence (no increment — TEE-02)
256	      //    - same CID (no repoint — TEE-01)
257	      //    - bytes differ (later EOL was written — TEE-01)
258	      expect(renewedRecord.sequence).toBe(originalRecord.sequence);
259	      expect(renewedRecord.value).toBe(originalRecord.value);
260	      expect(renewedBytes!.equals(originalBytes!)).toBe(false);
261	    }, 120_000);
262	
263	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
264	    // Test B — tombstone never re-signed
265	    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
266	    it('Test B: tombstoned name is never re-signed forward', async () => {
267	      const bob = fixture.accounts.get('bob')!;
268	      const bobCtx = bob.client.getContext();
269	
270	      // 1. Read TEE keys from the live DB
271	      const teeKeys = await readTeeKeys();
272	
273	      // 2. Create a subfolder enrolled for TEE republishing
274	      const { ipnsPrivateKey: subfolderKey } = await createSubfolder({
275	        name: 'tee-republish-test-b',
276	        ctx: bobCtx,
277	        teeKeys,
278	      });
279	      const ipnsName = await deriveIpnsName(deriveEd25519PublicKey(subfolderKey));
280	
281	      // 3. Capture the original signed_record bytes before tombstoning
282	      const originalBytes = await fetchSignedRecord(ipnsName);
283	      expect(originalBytes).not.toBeNull();
284	
285	      // 4. Tombstone the name via the API (unenrolls from republish schedule)
286	      await tombstoneName(bob.accessToken, ipnsName);
287	
288	      // 5. Assert the schedule row is gone (tombstone handler calls unenrollIpns)
289	      const rowExists = await scheduleRowExists(ipnsName);
290	      expect(rowExists).toBe(false);
291	
292	      // 6. Attempt to make the (now-absent) schedule row due — noop
293	      await makeScheduleDue(ipnsName);
294	
295	      // 7. Enqueue ONE republish-batch job
296	      await enqueueRepublishBatch();
297	
298	      // 8. Wait a moment for the batch to process
299	      //    The tombstoned name should be absent from getDueEntries (tombstoned_at IS NULL filter)
300	      //    so the signed_record should remain unchanged even after a full batch cycle.
301	      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 3_000));
302	
303	      // 9. Assert the signed_record is unchanged (tombstoned name was not re-signed)
304	      const currentBytes = await fetchSignedRecord(ipnsName);
305	      expect(currentBytes).not.toBeNull();
306	      expect(currentBytes!.equals(originalBytes!)).toBe(true);
307	    }, 120_000);
308	  }
309	);
310
```

```
1	# CipherBox TEE Worker - Multi-stage Docker build
2	# Build from repo root: docker build -f apps/tee-worker/Dockerfile -t cipherbox-tee-worker .
3	
4	# Stage 1: Install dependencies and build TypeScript
5	FROM node:20-alpine AS build
6	RUN corepack enable && corepack prepare pnpm@10 --activate
7	WORKDIR /app
8	COPY pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml package.json ./
9	COPY apps/tee-worker/package.json apps/tee-worker/
10	COPY packages/crypto/package.json packages/crypto/
11	COPY packages/core/package.json packages/core/
12	COPY packages/sdk-core/package.json packages/sdk-core/
13	COPY packages/api-client/package.json packages/api-client/
14	RUN pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --filter cipherbox-tee-worker...
15	COPY apps/tee-worker/ apps/tee-worker/
16	COPY packages/crypto/ packages/crypto/
17	COPY packages/core/ packages/core/
18	COPY packages/sdk-core/ packages/sdk-core/
19	COPY packages/api-client/ packages/api-client/
20	COPY tsconfig.base.json ./
21	RUN pnpm --filter @cipherbox/crypto build && pnpm --filter @cipherbox/core build && pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api-client build && pnpm --filter @cipherbox/sdk-core build
22	RUN pnpm --filter cipherbox-tee-worker build
23	
24	# Create standalone deployment (flat node_modules, no symlinks)
25	RUN pnpm --filter cipherbox-tee-worker deploy /deploy --prod --legacy
26	
27	# Stage 2: Production runtime
28	FROM node:20-alpine
29	RUN addgroup -g 1001 -S appgroup && adduser -S appuser -u 1001 -G appgroup
30	WORKDIR /app
31	COPY --from=build /deploy/node_modules ./node_modules
32	COPY --from=build /app/apps/tee-worker/dist ./dist
33	COPY --from=build /deploy/package.json ./package.json
34	
35	ENV NODE_ENV=production
36	ENV PORT=3001
37	EXPOSE 3001
38	USER appuser
39	CMD ["node", "dist/index.js"]
40	
```

```
226	          STATUS=$?
227	          set -e
228	
229	          # Check if migration:generate failed before inspecting output files
230	          if [ "$STATUS" -ne 0 ]; then
231	            if echo "$OUTPUT" | grep -q "No changes in database schema were found"; then
232	              echo "No entity/migration drift detected."
233	              exit 0
234	            fi
235	            echo "::error::migration:generate failed unexpectedly."

237	            exit 1

240	          if ls src/migrations/*DriftCheck*.ts 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then
241	            # Our hand-written migrations use explicit constraint/index names, but TypeORM
242	            # expects auto-generated hash-based names. Filter out cosmetic renames and only
243	            # fail on real structural drift (new/dropped tables, added/removed columns, type changes).
244	            if grep -qE 'CREATE TABLE|DROP TABLE|ADD COLUMN|DROP COLUMN|ALTER.*TYPE|RENAME COLUMN' src/migrations/*DriftCheck*.ts; then
245	              echo "::error::Entity/migration drift detected! Entities define schema changes not covered by migrations."
246	              echo ""
247	              echo "Generated migration contents:"

249	              echo ""
250	              echo "Fix: Run 'pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api typeorm migration:generate src/migrations/<Name> -d src/data-source.ts' and commit the result."
251	              exit 1
252	            else
253	              echo "Only cosmetic constraint/index naming differences detected (not structural drift). OK."
254	            fi
255	          else
256	            echo "No entity/migration drift detected."
257	          fi
```

```
1	/**
2	 * CipherBox TEE Worker
3	 *
4	 * Standalone Express HTTP server for Phala Cloud CVM deployment.
5	 * Receives encrypted IPNS private keys, decrypts with epoch-derived keys,
6	 * signs IPNS records, and returns signed records.
7	 *
8	 * Routes:
9	 *   GET  /health           - Public health check
10	 *   GET  /metrics          - Prometheus metrics (public, no auth)
11	 *   GET  /public-key       - TEE public key per epoch (auth required)
12	 *   POST /republish        - Batch IPNS signing (auth required)
13	 *   POST /migrate          - Batch CID migration between providers (auth required)
14	 *   POST /connection-test  - Server-side IPFS endpoint connection test (auth required)
15	 */
16	
17	import express from 'express';
18	import { authMiddleware } from './middleware/auth.js';
19	import { metricsMiddleware } from './middleware/metrics.js';
20	import { logger } from './services/logger.js';
21	import healthRouter from './routes/health.js';
22	import metricsRouter from './routes/metrics.js';
23	import publicKeyRouter from './routes/public-key.js';
24	import republishRouter from './routes/republish.js';
25	import migrateRouter from './routes/migrate.js';
26	import connectionTestRouter from './routes/connection-test.js';
27	
28	const app = express();
29	const port = parseInt(process.env.PORT || '3001', 10);
30	const mode = process.env.TEE_MODE || 'simulator';
31	
32	// JSON body parsing with 10mb limit for batch requests
33	app.use(express.json({ limit: '10mb' }));
34	
35	// Prometheus HTTP metrics (before route handlers, after JSON parsing)
36	app.use(metricsMiddleware);
37	
38	// Public routes (no auth)
39	app.use(healthRouter);
40	app.use(metricsRouter);
41	
42	// Protected routes (auth required)
43	app.use(authMiddleware, publicKeyRouter);
44	app.use(authMiddleware, republishRouter);
45	app.use(authMiddleware, migrateRouter);
46	app.use(authMiddleware, connectionTestRouter);
47	
48	app.listen(port, () => {
49	  logger.info('TEE worker started', { port, mode });
50	});
51	
52	export default app;
53	
```

```
1	/**
2	 * TEE Key Derivation Service
3	 *
4	 * Derives deterministic secp256k1 keypairs per epoch.
5	 * - Simulator mode: HKDF-SHA256 from a fixed seed (development/testing)
6	 * - CVM mode: DstackClient.getKey() for hardware-backed derivation (production)
7	 */
8	
9	import { hkdf } from '@noble/hashes/hkdf';
10	import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
11	import * as secp from '@noble/secp256k1';
12	
13	/** Cache public keys per epoch to avoid repeated derivation */
14	const publicKeyCache = new Map<number, Uint8Array>();
15	const MAX_CACHE_SIZE = 100;
16	
17	/** Valid epoch range — epochs are small sequential integers (one per ~4 weeks) */
18	export const MIN_EPOCH = 1;
19	export const MAX_EPOCH = 10_000;
20	
21	/**
22	 * 4-week epoch duration in milliseconds.
23	 * Must match the relay's epoch schedule configuration.
24	 */
25	export const EPOCH_DURATION_MS = 4 * 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
26	
27	/**
28	 * Derive the current epoch from the TEE's own clock.
29	 *
30	 * Reads EPOCH_ZERO_TIMESTAMP_MS from the environment at call time (not module
31	 * load) so tests and staged rollouts can vary it without restarting the process.
32	 * Returns MIN_EPOCH (1) when the anchor is absent or in the future — a safe
33	 * fallback floor that never triggers the stale-key guard.
34	 *
35	 * This is the §6.7-1 internal-clock derivation — it never reads a relay-supplied
36	 * currentEpoch/previousEpoch scalar.
37	 *
38	 * @returns Current epoch number (≥ MIN_EPOCH)
39	 */
40	export function getInternalCurrentEpoch(): number {
41	  const anchor = parseInt(process.env.EPOCH_ZERO_TIMESTAMP_MS ?? '0', 10);
42	  // Treat unset (0), malformed (NaN), and non-positive anchors identically: fall
43	  // back to MIN_EPOCH before any Date.now() math so a bad env var can never
44	  // propagate a NaN epoch into the stale-key guard.
45	  if (!Number.isFinite(anchor) || anchor <= 0) {
46	    return MIN_EPOCH; // Safe fallback: no valid anchor configured → epoch 1
47	  }
48	  return Math.max(MIN_EPOCH, Math.floor((Date.now() - anchor) / EPOCH_DURATION_MS) + 1);
49	}
50	
51	/**
52	 * Derive a deterministic secp256k1 keypair for a given epoch.
53	 *
54	 * In simulator mode, uses HKDF-SHA256 with a fixed seed.
55	 * In CVM mode, uses Phala dstack SDK for hardware-backed derivation.
56	 *
57	 * @param epoch - The key epoch number
58	 * @returns Object with publicKey (65 bytes, uncompressed) and privateKey (32 bytes)
59	 */
60	export async function getKeypair(
61	  epoch: number
62	): Promise<{ publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array }> {
63	  const mode = process.env.TEE_MODE || 'simulator';
64	
65	  const env = process.env.CIPHERBOX_ENVIRONMENT;
66	  if (
67	    mode === 'simulator' &&
68	    (env === 'production' || (!env && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production'))
69	  ) {
70	    throw new Error(
71	      'TEE_MODE=simulator is not allowed in production. Set TEE_MODE=cvm for production deployments, or set CIPHERBOX_ENVIRONMENT explicitly.'
72	    );
73	  }
74	
75	  let privateKey: Uint8Array;
76	
77	  if (mode === 'cvm') {
78	    // Production: Phala Cloud CVM with dstack SDK
79	    // Dynamic import -- @phala/dstack-sdk is only available inside CVM
80	    const { DstackClient } = await import('@phala/dstack-sdk');
81	    const client = new DstackClient();
82	    const keyResult = await client.getKey('cipherbox/ipns-republish', `epoch-${epoch}`);
83	
84	    // Defensive handling: SDK v0.5+ returns { key: Uint8Array },
85	    // older versions returned { asUint8Array(): Uint8Array }
86	    const keyAny = keyResult as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
87	    const rawKey =
88	      'key' in keyResult && keyResult.key instanceof Uint8Array
89	        ? keyResult.key
90	        : typeof keyAny.asUint8Array === 'function'
91	          ? (keyAny.asUint8Array as () => Uint8Array)()
92	          : (() => {
93	              throw new Error('Unexpected DstackClient.getKey() return type');
94	            })();
95	    privateKey = new Uint8Array(rawKey.slice(0, 32));
96	  } else {
97	    // Simulator: deterministic HKDF derivation from fixed seed
98	    const seed = new TextEncoder().encode('cipherbox-tee-simulator-seed');
99	    const salt = new TextEncoder().encode('cipherbox-dev');
100	    const info = new TextEncoder().encode(`epoch-${epoch}`);
101	    privateKey = hkdf(sha256, seed, salt, info, 32);
102	  }
103	
104	  // Derive uncompressed public key (65 bytes, 0x04 prefix)
105	  const publicKey = secp.getPublicKey(privateKey, false);
106	
107	  // Cache public key for this epoch (bounded to prevent memory DoS)
108	  if (publicKeyCache.size >= MAX_CACHE_SIZE) {
109	    const firstKey = publicKeyCache.keys().next().value;
110	    if (firstKey !== undefined) publicKeyCache.delete(firstKey);
111	  }
112	  publicKeyCache.set(epoch, publicKey);
113	
114	  return { publicKey, privateKey };
115	}
116	
117	/**
118	 * Get the cached public key for an epoch, or derive it.
119	 *
120	 * @param epoch - The key epoch number
121	 * @returns 65-byte uncompressed secp256k1 public key
122	 */
123	export async function getPublicKey(epoch: number): Promise<Uint8Array> {
124	  const cached = publicKeyCache.get(epoch);
125	  if (cached) {
126	    return cached;
127	  }
128	
129	  const { publicKey } = await getKeypair(epoch);
130	  return publicKey;
131	}
132
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Shared Secret Authentication Middleware
3	 *
4	 * Validates requests against the TEE_WORKER_SECRET environment variable.
5	 * Uses Bearer token authentication in the Authorization header.
6	 */
7	
8	import { timingSafeEqual as cryptoTimingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
9	import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from 'express';
10	
11	/**
12	 * Express middleware that validates the Authorization: Bearer <secret> header.
13	 *
14	 * Compares the provided token against TEE_WORKER_SECRET env var.
15	 * Returns 401 if missing or mismatched.
16	 */
17	export function authMiddleware(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {
18	  const expectedSecret = process.env.TEE_WORKER_SECRET;
19	
20	  if (!expectedSecret) {
21	    res.status(500).json({ error: 'TEE_WORKER_SECRET not configured' });
22	    return;
23	  }
24	
25	  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
26	  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
27	    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Missing or invalid Authorization header' });
28	    return;
29	  }
30	
31	  const token = authHeader.slice(7); // Remove 'Bearer ' prefix
32	
33	  // Constant-time comparison to prevent timing attacks
34	  if (
35	    token.length !== expectedSecret.length ||
36	    !cryptoTimingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(token), Buffer.from(expectedSecret))
37	  ) {
38	    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid authentication token' });
39	    return;
40	  }
41	
42	  next();
43	}
44	
```

```json
{
```
```json
  "replace_all": false,
```

```json
  "old_string": "          if ! curl -fsS http://localhost:3001/health > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n            echo \"Mock IPNS routing failed to start within 15s\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n      - name: Start API server",
  "new_string": "          if ! curl -fsS http://localhost:3001/health > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n            echo \"Mock IPNS routing failed to start within 15s\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n      # Start the TEE worker (simulator mode) BEFORE the API. The API's TeeModule\n      # onModuleInit runs exactly once at boot and only populates tee_key_state if the\n      # worker is reachable AND the Bearer secret matches — so the worker must be up\n      # first, or current_public_key stays empty and the tee-republish suite fails.\n      # Simulator mode derives keys via HKDF (no dstack socket / CVM hardware needed).\n      - name: Start TEE worker (simulator)\n        run: |\n          pnpm --filter cipherbox-tee-worker start &\n          for i in $(seq 1 20); do\n            if curl -fsS http://localhost:3002/health > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n              echo \"TEE worker is ready\"\n              break\n            fi\n            sleep 1\n          done\n          if ! curl -fsS http://localhost:3002/health > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n            echo \"TEE worker failed to start within 20s\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n        env:\n          PORT: 3002\n          TEE_MODE: simulator\n          CIPHERBOX_ENVIRONMENT: development\n          TEE_WORKER_SECRET=[REDACTED]\n\n      - name: Start API server"
}
```

```
86	    );
87	  }
88	});
```

```
1	---
2	created: 2026-06-29
3	title: createSubfolder accepts teeKeys but does not wire encryptedIpnsPrivateKey/keyEpoch — fail closed or wire TEE republish
4	area: sdk-core
5	resolves_phase: 67
6	files:
7	  - packages/sdk-core/src/folder/registration.ts
8	---
9	
10	## Problem
11	
12	Flagged Major (Security & Privacy) by the Phase-63 PR CodeRabbit review (`registration.ts:42-101`, an outside-diff body comment).
13	
14	`createSubfolder` accepts `teeKeys` (the TEE-encrypted IPNS private key + key epoch used so the TEE worker can republish the subfolder's IPNS record), but the publish path does not set `encryptedIpnsPrivateKey`/`keyEpoch`, and the function returns them unset. A new subfolder created this way would therefore not be picked up by the TEE republisher and its IPNS record would eventually expire.
15	
16	Phase 63 is the read-chain/rotation skeleton and intentionally does not wire TEE republishing (TEE lease-renewer contract is Phase 67; the app is non-runnable mid-milestone). The risk is silent: a caller that supplies `teeKeys` expecting republish wiring gets a subfolder that is not republish-enrolled, with no error.
17	
18	## Solution
19	
20	Either:
21	- **Fail closed now (cheap guard):** if `teeKeys` are supplied to `createSubfolder` but the publish path does not yet wire them, throw a clear "TEE republish wiring not implemented (Phase 67)" error rather than silently publishing without them — so no caller mistakenly believes a subfolder is republish-enrolled. (Low-risk; can be done before Phase 67 if any caller starts passing teeKeys.)
22	- **Wire it (Phase 67):** thread `encryptedIpnsPrivateKey` + `keyEpoch` into the published record and the return value as part of the TEE lease-renewer integration.
23	
24	Verify whether any current caller passes `teeKeys` (grep) — if none do, this is latent param surface and the fail-closed guard is sufficient interim hardening.
25	
26	## References
27	
28	- CodeRabbit PR #579 review, `registration.ts:42-101`
29	- CLAUDE.md TEE Republishing architecture; ROADMAP Phase 67 (TEE lease-renewer contract)
30	
```

> AGENT

The full chain is validated locally with my exact env values:
- Worker starts **headless** (`node dist/index.js`), log confirms `mode:simulator`, `port:3099` — no dstack socket/DB/redis.
- `/health` → `{healthy:true, mode:simulator, epoch:1}` (fresh, uptime 1s) — exactly what the API's `initializeFromTee` reads.
- `/public-key?epoch=1` → 401 without auth, and with matching Bearer returns a valid 65-byte secp256k1 pubkey (`04...`, 130 hex) — what gets written to `tee_key_state.current_public_key`.

The novel/risky part (headless simulator worker) is proven. Now let me run a final adversarial review of the complete diff against the requirements, since CI mistakes only surface remotely.

```
1	---
2	name: project-tee-republish-e2e-stack-recipe
3	description: Concrete local-stack recipe to run the sdk-e2e TEE republish round-trip (phase 67+); the fiddly wiring that took a full debug session

9	
10	Running `tests/sdk-e2e/src/suites/tee-republish.test.ts` (the real relay→TEE→DB IPNS
11	lease-renew round-trip) needs ALL of this wired consistently, or it fails in confusing ways.
12	Learned the hard way in Phase 67 (2026-07-01).
13	
14	**DB is `cipherbox`, not `cipherbox_test`.** `apps/api/.env` may carry a stray
15	`DB_DATABASE=cipherbox_test` (a CI-only name), but the running API defaults to `cipherbox`
16	(`app.module.ts` `configService.get('DB_DATABASE','cipherbox')`). Two docker postgres exist:
17	`cipherbox-postgres` (host **5432**) and `irl-network-postgres` (host 5433). Force
18	`DB_DATABASE=cipherbox` for the migration, the API, AND the e2e (the suite defaults to
19	`cipherbox` but pass it explicitly). The TypeORM migration CLI (`data-source.ts`) reads `.env`,
20	so `pnpm migration:run` alone hits the stray `cipherbox_test` — override:
21	`DB_DATABASE=cipherbox pnpm migration:run`.
22	
23	**Rebuild the tee-worker; it is usually weeks-stale.** `docker compose ps` may not even list it
24	(the running container often predates the compose `tee-worker` service). Always
25	`docker compose -f docker/docker-compose.yml up -d --build tee-worker`. It binds host **3002**
26	→ container 3001 (host 3001 is `mock-ipns-routing`). Simulator TEE key is DETERMINISTIC (HKDF
27	from fixed seed `cipherbox-tee-simulator-seed`), so a rebuild keeps the same key and existing
28	`tee_key_state` stays valid.
29	
30	**Restart the API from CURRENT source.** It runs as `node dist/main` (NOT nest watch), so a
31	running instance serves stale compiled code — `pnpm --filter @cipherbox/api build`, kill the
32	:3000 node, then start with the full env:
33	`DB_DATABASE=cipherbox TEE_WORKER_URL=http://localhost:3002 TEE_WORKER_SECRET=dev-secret TEST_LOGIN_SECRET=[REDACTED] node dist/main`.
34	Inline env wins over `.env` (dotenv no-override). On startup `TeeService.initializeFromTee()`
35	populates `tee_key_state` from the worker (only when the worker is healthy + the bearer secret
36	matches — `/republish` and `/public-key` are behind `TEE_WORKER_SECRET` auth; `/health` is open).
37	
38	**Secrets must match on both sides.** `TEST_LOGIN_SECRET` (API) must equal the harness default
39	`e2e-test-secret-do-not-use-in-production` (or set `SDK_E2E_SECRET` to the API's value) or every
40	account create 401s. `TEE_WORKER_SECRET` API↔worker must match (`dev-secret`).
41	
42	**The suite reads `tee_key_state.current_public_key` (bytea) + `current_epoch`** — there is NO
43	`public_key` column; return the bytea as hex for `createSubfolder`'s `hexToBytes`. Redis is
44	**6380**. Determinism: direct-pg `UPDATE ... next_republish_at = NOW() - interval` + ONE
45	`queue.add('republish-batch', {})` on the `republish` queue — no cron/timer wait.
46	
47	**LOCAL-ONLY — gated out of CI (Phase 67 ship, 2026-07-01).** The suite is
48	`describe.skipIf(!!process.env.CI)` with the module-level `beforeAll` DB query also guarded
49	by that flag. CI's `sdk-e2e` job (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) provisions NO TEE worker and
50	uses `cipherbox_test` (it does not export `DB_DATABASE` to the e2e step), so running this
51	suite in CI fails with `database "cipherbox" does not exist`. It is the documented LOCAL
52	publish gate — run it locally (recipe above) before merging TEE/IPNS-lifecycle changes; CI
53	correctly skips it (2 skipped, suite green). Todo filed to optionally add a CI TEE worker.
54	
55	See [[project-sdk-e2e-only-cross-package-publish-gate]] (the general gate + secret trap) and
56	[[project-staging-vps-access]].
```

> AGENT

Implementation complete and verified. Here's the summary.

## What changed

**`.github/workflows/ci.yml`** (`sdk-e2e` job) — provisions the full TEE stack (option 1 from the todo):
- Build the tee-worker in the "Build packages" step.
- New **"Start TEE worker (simulator)"** step runs it headless on `:3002` (`TEE_MODE=simulator`, `CIPHERBOX_ENVIRONMENT=development`, `TEE_WORKER_SECRET`), placed **before** the API.
- Wired `TEE_WORKER_URL=http://localhost:3002` + matching `TEE_WORKER_SECRET` onto the API (`.env` + start step).
- Exported `DB_DATABASE=cipherbox_test` and `REDIS_PORT=6379` onto the "Run SDK E2E tests" step so the suite's `DB_CONFIG`/`REDIS_CONFIG` hit the CI stack instead of the local `cipherbox`/`6380` defaults.

The schedule-collapse migration applies automatically via the API's `migrationsRun: true` — no separate migration step needed.

**`tests/sdk-e2e/src/suites/tee-republish.test.ts`** — dropped the `SKIP_TEE_LIVE = !!CI` gate (kept the migration-applied guard), updated the docblock.

**`.planning/todos/`** — moved the todo to `completed/` with a resolution note.

## The load-bearing detail

The API's `tee_key_state` bootstrap is a one-shot `TeeModule.onModuleInit` that **swallows errors** — so the worker must be up + reachable with a matching secret *before* the API boots, or the row stays empty silently and the suite throws "tee_key_state is empty". The worker step is ordered before the API step accordingly.

## Verification

- **Live headless smoke test**: worker runs via `node dist/index.js` in simulator mode (no dstack socket) — `/health` returns `{healthy, mode:simulator, epoch:1}`, `/public-key?epoch=1` is 401 without auth and returns a valid 65-byte secp256k1 key with the matching Bearer.
- **Adversarial diff review** (sub-agent): all 10 correctness checks pass (ordering, secret/port/redis/DB match, no dangling gate refs, build, env precedence, no collateral damage, simulator not rejected). No silent-pass risk — a broken round-trip surfaces as a red job.
- YAML valid (actionlint), test type-clean and lint-clean (auto-formatted).

Changes are on `feat/tee-lease-renewer-contract-rewrite`, **uncommitted**. Want me to commit them?

```
131	      // Build RepublishEntry payloads from the paired ipns_records row.
132	      // D-02 / TEE-03: ALL signing inputs come from the canonical ipns_records row —
133	      // no schedule snapshot fields, no relay-supplied epoch scalars (removed per D-03).
134	      const teeEntries: RepublishEntry[] = batch.map(({ schedule, record }) => ({
135	        encryptedIpnsKey: record.encryptedIpnsPrivateKey!.toString('base64'),
136	        keyEpoch: record.keyEpoch!,
137	        ipnsName: schedule.ipnsName,
138	        signedRecord: record.signedRecord!.toString('base64'),
139	      }));
140	
141	      let teeResults: RepublishResult[];
142	      try {
143	        teeResults = await this.teeService.republish(teeEntries);
144	      } catch (error) {
145	        // TEE unreachable -- mark entire batch as failed
146	        const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
147	        this.logger.error(`TEE worker unreachable: ${message}`);
148	
149	        for (const { schedule } of batch) {
150	          await this.handleEntryFailure(schedule, `TEE unreachable: ${message}`);
151	        }
152	        totalFailed += batch.length;
153	        continue;
154	      }
155	
156	      // Process results
157	      for (let j = 0; j < batch.length; j++) {
158	        const { schedule, record } = batch[j];
159	        const result = teeResults[j];
160	
161	        if (!result) {
162	          await this.handleEntryFailure(schedule, 'No result from TEE worker');
163	          totalFailed++;
164	          continue;
165	        }
166	
167	        // requiresReEnroll: IPNS key can no longer be decrypted; log and route as failure
168	        // (consumer surface deferred to Phase 68/69 — non-fatal here).
169	        if (result.requiresReEnroll) {
170	          this.logger.warn(
171	            `TEE requires re-enrollment for ${schedule.ipnsName} — routing as failure (Phase 68/69)`
172	          );
173	          await this.handleEntryFailure(schedule, 'TEE requires re-enrollment');
174	          totalFailed++;
175	          continue;
176	        }
177	
178	        if (result.success && result.signedRecord && result.newSequenceNumber) {
179	          // Defense-in-depth (TEE-02): the TEE must re-sign the SAME sequence. If it
180	          // returned a different sequence, publishing would push delegated routing ahead
181	          // of the canonical ipns_records row (whose renewal CAS only matches the loaded
182	          // sequence), leaving the two out of sync. Reject instead of publishing.
183	          if (result.newSequenceNumber !== record.sequenceNumber) {
184	            await this.handleEntryFailure(
185	              schedule,
186	              `TEE returned unexpected sequence ${result.newSequenceNumber}; expected ${record.sequenceNumber}`
187	            );
188	            totalFailed++;
189	            continue;
190	          }
191	
192	          // TEE signing succeeded -- now publish to delegated routing
193	          try {
194	            await this.publishSignedRecord(schedule.ipnsName, result.signedRecord);
195	
196	            // Update schedule entry: ONLY scheduling fields survive (no crypto columns).
197	            schedule.lastRepublishAt = new Date();
198	            schedule.consecutiveFailures = 0;
199	            schedule.status = 'active';
200	            schedule.lastError = null;
201	            schedule.nextRepublishAt = this.nextRepublishTime();
202	
203	            await this.scheduleRepository.save(schedule);
204	
205	            // EOL-only renewal write: update signed_record in ipns_records via
206	            // equality CAS. Uses the sequence number carried from getDueEntries
207	            // (NOT result.newSequenceNumber as a write target — seq is unchanged).
208	            // §6.6 / TEE-04: WHERE sequence_number = :loaded AND tombstoned_at IS NULL
209	            await this.renewIpnsRecordEol(
210	              schedule.ipnsName,
211	              record.sequenceNumber, // loaded seq from the batch
212	              Buffer.from(result.signedRecord, 'base64')
213	            );
214	
215	            // Epoch upgrade: re-encrypted key + new epoch go to ipns_records (not schedule).
216	            if (
217	              result.upgradedEncryptedKey !== undefined &&
218	              result.upgradedKeyEpoch !== undefined
219	            ) {
220	              this.logger.log(
221	                `Epoch upgrade for ${schedule.ipnsName}: epoch ${record.keyEpoch} -> ${result.upgradedKeyEpoch}`
222	              );
223	              // Scope the upgrade to the owner's non-tombstoned row at the loaded epoch:
224	              // - tombstonedAt: IsNull() preserves tombstone immutability (never re-encrypt
225	              //   a tombstoned row, even if it was tombstoned after the batch loaded)
226	              // - userId pins the owner (ipnsName uniqueness is app-level, not a DB constraint)
227	              // - keyEpoch equality is a CAS so a concurrent key rotation is not clobbered
228	              await this.ipnsRecordRepository.update(
229	                {
230	                  ipnsName: schedule.ipnsName,
231	                  userId: record.userId,
232	                  tombstonedAt: IsNull(),
233	                  keyEpoch: record.keyEpoch!, // non-null: getDueEntries filters keyEpoch IS NOT NULL
234	                },
235	                {
236	                  encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: Buffer.from(result.upgradedEncryptedKey, 'base64'),
237	                  keyEpoch: result.upgradedKeyEpoch,
238	                }
239	              );
```

```
1	---
2	created: 2026-07-01T00:00:00.000Z
3	title: Harden TEE republish write-path error handling and epoch-upgrade concurrency
4	area: correctness
5	severity: low
6	files:
7	  - apps/api/src/republish/republish.service.ts
8	  - apps/tee-worker/src/services/key-manager.ts
9	  - apps/tee-worker/src/routes/republish.ts
10	---
11	
12	> Deferred from the Phase 67 ship (CodeRabbit). These touch the republish write path and
13	> change error-handling semantics — deliberately deferred because a naive fix risks
14	> behavior regressions (over-escalating failures, masking network-publish success) and
15	> each needs a dedicated test. The phase's happy path, security invariants, and live E2E
16	> round-trip are all verified.
17	>
18	> NOTE: the original CodeRabbit/greptile epoch-upgrade finding (unconditional
19	> `ipnsRecordRepository.update`) was FIXED during the ship — the write now scopes to
20	> `{ ipnsName, userId, tombstonedAt: IsNull(), keyEpoch: <loaded> }` (tombstone immutability
21	> + owner scope + epoch CAS), and `getDueEntries` now filters `signedRecord`/`keyEpoch`
22	> non-null. The items below remain.
23	
24	## Findings
25	
26	### 1. `renewIpnsRecordEol` swallows real DB errors as success (`republish.service.ts` ~421-442)
27	
28	The `catch` treats every failure as non-fatal (logs `warn`, returns). The `affected === 0`
29	CAS-miss is genuinely harmless, but a real DB error (connection drop, constraint) is
30	masked and the entry is still counted as `succeeded`. Current behavior is intentional
31	(the network publish already succeeded; the DB row self-heals next cycle by re-renewing
32	the same CID+seq), but real DB errors should at least surface at `error` level or as a
33	distinct metric so silent write-back failures are observable. Keep the CAS-miss path
34	non-fatal; distinguish it from real exceptions.
35	
36	### 2. Key-decrypt fallback masks config/infra errors as "corrupted key" (`key-manager.ts` ~90-109)
37	
38	`decryptWithFallback`'s two trials use bare `catch {}`, so a `getKeypair()` failure
39	(e.g. epoch out of MIN/MAX range, simulator-in-production guard) is swallowed and
40	reported as the generic "key may be corrupted or from an unknown epoch". Only the
41	expected unwrap/epoch-mismatch failure should advance to the next trial; rethrow (or
42	wrap with cause) other exceptions from `getKeypair()`/`decryptIpnsKey()` so a
43	deployment misconfiguration is not misdiagnosed.
44	
45	### 3. Per-entry null guard in the republish route (`republish.ts` ~94-99, ~180)
46	
47	A `null`/non-object `entry` makes `entry.signedRecord` throw, and the `catch` then
48	dereferences `entry.ipnsName` — throwing again and crashing the whole batch (500).
49	The relay is trusted and never sends null entries, so this is defense-in-depth: validate
50	each `entry` is a non-null object at the top of the loop and skip/serialize invalid
51	items safely in the failure path.
52
```

```
440	   * §6.6 / TEE-04 carryover: uses the same equality-CAS shape as the
441	   * Phase-66 publish gate (sequence_number = :expected AND tombstoned_at IS NULL)
442	   * but does NOT change sequence_number — only signed_record and updatedAt are
443	   * updated (lease-renewer: same CID, same seq, later EOL bytes).
444	   *
445	   * On affected === 0: a forward publish raced the batch and already advanced
446	   * the sequence (or the row was tombstoned at the write level). Either way
447	   * the renewal is harmlessly discarded — the newer row already has a valid EOL.
448	   */
449	  private async renewIpnsRecordEol(
450	    ipnsName: string,
451	    loadedSequenceNumber: string,
452	    renewedSignedRecord: Buffer
453	  ): Promise<void> {
454	    try {
455	      const result = await this.ipnsRecordRepository
456	        .createQueryBuilder()
457	        .update(IpnsRecord)
458	        .set({ signedRecord: renewedSignedRecord, updatedAt: new Date() })
459	        .where('ipns_name = :ipnsName AND sequence_number = :expected AND tombstoned_at IS NULL', {
460	          ipnsName,
461	          expected: loadedSequenceNumber,
462	        })
463	        .execute();
464	
465	      if (result.affected === 0) {
466	        // Forward publish raced (seq advanced) OR tombstoned at the write level.
467	        // Either case: the renewal is harmlessly discarded.
468	        this.logger.debug(
469	          `EOL renewal CAS miss for ${ipnsName} (seq advanced or tombstoned) — discarding`
470	        );
471	      }
472	    } catch (error) {
473	      // Non-fatal: log and continue. The IPNS publish already succeeded.
474	      const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
475	      this.logger.warn(`renewIpnsRecordEol failed for ${ipnsName}: ${message}`);
476	    }
477	  }
478	
479	  /**
480	   * Calculate next republish time (now + 6 hours).
481	   */
482	  private nextRepublishTime(): Date {
483	    return new Date(Date.now() + REPUBLISH_INTERVAL_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000);
484	  }
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/apps/api/src/republish/republish.service.ts",
  "old_string": "    const ipnsNames = schedules.map((s) => s.ipnsName);\n    const records = await this.ipnsRecordRepository.find({\n      where: {\n        ipnsName: In(ipnsNames),\n        tombstonedAt: IsNull(),\n        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: Not(IsNull()),\n        // Also require the signing inputs so the teeEntries map below cannot deref a\n        // null with `!`. A row missing either drops out here (re-picked next cycle)\n        // instead of throwing outside the inner try and aborting the whole batch.\n        signedRecord: Not(IsNull()),\n        keyEpoch: Not(IsNull()),\n      },\n    });\n\n    const recordMap = new Map(records.map((r) => [r.ipnsName, r]));\n\n    return schedules\n      .map((schedule) => {\n        const record = recordMap.get(schedule.ipnsName);\n        if (!record) return null; // race: tombstoned or key removed between the two queries\n        return { schedule, record };\n      })\n      .filter((p): p is DueEntryPair => p !== null);",
  "new_string": "    // Pair schedule rows to ipns_records by BOTH userId and ipnsName. ipnsName\n    // uniqueness is app-level, not a DB constraint (see the epoch-upgrade write\n    // below), so keying the pairing on ipnsName alone could pair one user's schedule\n    // with another user's record if two rows ever share a name. Scope by userId.\n    const ipnsNames = [...new Set(schedules.map((s) => s.ipnsName))];\n    const userIds = [...new Set(schedules.map((s) => s.userId))];\n    const records = await this.ipnsRecordRepository.find({\n      where: {\n        userId: In(userIds),\n        ipnsName: In(ipnsNames),\n        tombstonedAt: IsNull(),\n        encryptedIpnsPrivateKey: Not(IsNull()),\n        // Also require the signing inputs so the teeEntries map below cannot deref a\n        // null with `!`. A row missing either drops out here (re-picked next cycle)\n        // instead of throwing outside the inner try and aborting the whole batch.\n        signedRecord: Not(IsNull()),\n        keyEpoch: Not(IsNull()),\n      },\n    });\n\n    const pairKey = (userId: string, ipnsName: string) => `${userId}:${ipnsName}`;\n    const recordMap = new Map(records.map((r) => [pairKey(r.userId, r.ipnsName), r]));\n\n    return schedules\n      .map((schedule) => {\n        const record = recordMap.get(pairKey(schedule.userId, schedule.ipnsName));\n        if (!record) return null; // race: tombstoned or key removed between the two queries\n        return { schedule, record };\n      })\n      .filter((p): p is DueEntryPair => p !== null);"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "   * §6.6 / TEE-04 carryover: uses the same equality-CAS shape as the\n   * Phase-66 publish gate (sequence_number = :expected AND tombstoned_at IS NULL)\n   * but does NOT change sequence_number — only signed_record and updatedAt are\n   * updated (lease-renewer: same CID, same seq, later EOL bytes).\n   *\n   * On affected === 0: a forward publish raced the batch and already advanced\n   * the sequence (or the row was tombstoned at the write level). Either way\n   * the renewal is harmlessly discarded — the newer row already has a valid EOL.\n   */\n  private async renewIpnsRecordEol(\n    ipnsName: string,\n    loadedSequenceNumber: string,\n    renewedSignedRecord: Buffer\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      const result = await this.ipnsRecordRepository\n        .createQueryBuilder()\n        .update(IpnsRecord)\n        .set({ signedRecord: renewedSignedRecord, updatedAt: new Date() })\n        .where('ipns_name = :ipnsName AND sequence_number = :expected AND tombstoned_at IS NULL', {\n          ipnsName,\n          expected: loadedSequenceNumber,\n        })\n        .execute();",
  "new_string": "   * §6.6 / TEE-04 carryover: uses the same equality-CAS shape as the\n   * Phase-66 publish gate (sequence_number = :expected AND tombstoned_at IS NULL),\n   * additionally scoped by user_id (ipnsName uniqueness is app-level, not a DB\n   * constraint — so the CAS must pin the owner), but does NOT change sequence_number\n   * — only signed_record and updatedAt are updated (lease-renewer: same CID, same\n   * seq, later EOL bytes).\n   *\n   * On affected === 0: a forward publish raced the batch and already advanced\n   * the sequence (or the row was tombstoned at the write level). Either way\n   * the renewal is harmlessly discarded — the newer row already has a valid EOL.\n   */\n  private async renewIpnsRecordEol(\n    ipnsName: string,\n    userId: string,\n    loadedSequenceNumber: string,\n    renewedSignedRecord: Buffer\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      const result = await this.ipnsRecordRepository\n        .createQueryBuilder()\n        .update(IpnsRecord)\n        .set({ signedRecord: renewedSignedRecord, updatedAt: new Date() })\n        .where(\n          'ipns_name = :ipnsName AND user_id = :userId AND sequence_number = :expected AND tombstoned_at IS NULL',\n          {\n            ipnsName,\n            userId,\n            expected: loadedSequenceNumber,\n          }\n        )\n        .execute();"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "### 3. Per-entry null guard in the republish route (`republish.ts` ~94-99, ~180)\n\nA `null`/non-object `entry` makes `entry.signedRecord` throw, and the `catch` then\ndereferences `entry.ipnsName` — throwing again and crashing the whole batch (500).\nThe relay is trusted and never sends null entries, so this is defense-in-depth: validate\neach `entry` is a non-null object at the top of the loop and skip/serialize invalid\nitems safely in the failure path.",
  "new_string": "### 3. Per-entry null guard in the republish route (`republish.ts` ~94-99, ~180)\n\nA `null`/non-object `entry` makes `entry.signedRecord` throw, and the `catch` then\ndereferences `entry.ipnsName` — throwing again and crashing the whole batch (500).\nThe relay is trusted and never sends null entries, so this is defense-in-depth: validate\neach `entry` is a non-null object at the top of the loop and skip/serialize invalid\nitems safely in the failure path.\n\n### 4. Rename the `encryptedIpnsKey` wire field to `encryptedIpnsPrivateKey` (nitpick)\n\nCodeRabbit (PR #585, 2026-07-01): `RepublishEntry` sends the encrypted IPNS private key\nas `encryptedIpnsKey` (`republish.service.ts` ~135), but the repo terminology standard is\n`encryptedIpnsPrivateKey`. This is a **cross-package wire contract** — the same field name\nis read by the TEE worker (`apps/tee-worker/src/routes/republish.ts:58,121`) and referenced\nacross ~15 worker test sites (`apps/tee-worker/src/__tests__/republish.test.ts`). Deferred\nbecause it's a trivial-importance rename that must change the API sender, the worker\nconsumer, and all worker tests in one lockstep commit; not worth risking a wire-contract\ntouch inside review resolution. Do it as its own scoped change with the worker tests."
}
```

## Summary

The 10 inline review threads were already resolved. The only open items were **2 CodeRabbit "outside diff range" body comments** from the latest review (on commit `113bb5db8`), both in `apps/api/src/republish/republish.service.ts`:

| Finding | Severity | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `getDueEntries`/`renewIpnsRecordEol` keyed by `ipnsName` only → could pair/renew another owner's row | 🟠 Major (security) | **Fixed** in `a73c4d660` |
| Wire field `encryptedIpnsKey` should be `encryptedIpnsPrivateKey` | 🔵 Trivial nitpick | **Deferred** to hardening todo |

**Fix (`a73c4d660`):**
- `getDueEntries` (`republish.service.ts:74–96`) — record map now keyed by `(userId, ipnsName)` and the `ipns_records` query filtered by `userId`.
- `renewIpnsRecordEol` (`:449–463`) — `user_id = :userId` added to the equality CAS.
- Consistent with the epoch-upgrade write already scoped by `userId` during the ship (`ipnsName` uniqueness is app-level, not DB-enforced).
- Tests: cross-user records no longer pair; renewal CAS asserts `userId` — **40/40 republish specs pass**, `nest build` clean, eslint clean.

**Deferred:** the `encryptedIpnsKey` rename is a cross-package wire contract (API sender + tee-worker consumer at `republish.ts:58,121` + ~15 worker test sites) — recorded as finding #4 in `.planning/todos/pending/2026-07-01-tee-republish-writepath-error-handling-hardening.md` for its own atomic, lockstep change.

**Pushed** `113bb5db8..a73c4d660` and posted an [acknowledgment comment](https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/585#issuecomment-4854746510) on the PR. CodeRabbit will re-review the new commit and should confirm the Major finding resolved.

One note: the commit landed despite the tool reporting a 2-minute timeout — 1Password SSH signing was awaiting biometric approval, which completed as the wrapper timed out. I verified via `git log` rather than retrying (which would have risked a duplicate).

```
12	> Deferred from the Phase 67 ship (CodeRabbit, MAJOR). This is a cross-layer rename of
13	> the TEE↔relay wire contract, and the `encryptedIpnsKey` naming predates Phase 67 (the

15	> contract-wide → defer to a dedicated change with matching relay/test updates.

17	## Problem
18

20	`encrypted_ipns_key` as a name to avoid. The TEE republish contract still uses
21	`encryptedIpnsKey` (request `RepublishEntry`) and `upgradedEncryptedKey` (response
22	`RepublishResult`) in `apps/tee-worker/src/routes/republish.ts`.
23	
24	## Proposed fix
25	
26	Rename to `encryptedIpnsPrivateKey` (and e.g. `upgradedEncryptedIpnsPrivateKey`)
27	consistently across:
28	
29	- the TEE worker route request/response shapes,
30	- the API relay that builds the request and reads the response (`tee.service.ts` /
31	  `republish.service.ts`),
32	- the tee-worker + api tests.
33	
34	Preserve semantics (base64 ECIES ciphertext; re-encryption on epoch upgrade). Land it as
35	one atomic rename so the wire contract never drifts between layers.
36
```
